## 1. Per-leg production replay (`per-leg-replay`)
- New `replayExecuteBatchPerLeg`: replays the batch as `eth_call` self-call `executeBatch` prefixes (`[wrap]`, `[wrap, approve]`, `[wrap, approve, swap]`) against `getProductionBaseReadClient()` to pinpoint the failing leg, with full revert data + decoded inner selector (`extractExecutionFailedInnerSelector`) logged untruncated.
- `resolveSwapBatchExecutionRevertError` maps the failing leg to existing user-facing copy (Permit2 `InvalidNonce`, slippage, insufficient funds) via the `error.ts` mappings.
- Wired into both the **estimate advisory-skip path** (replay before blindly skipping on a swap-heavy multi-call execution revert) and the **final send error path** (replay before falling back to the generic "Execution reverted for an unknown reason").
- Addresses are normalized via `getAddress(lowercase)` so doc-form non-EIP-55 casing (e.g. the canonical CSW literal) never aborts the replay.

## 2. Retry-ladder abort (`abort-ladder`)
- `shouldAbortUserOpGasLadder` classifies deterministic execution reverts and `PreflightSimulationRejectionError` as non-retryable — the verificationGasLimit ladder (400k→5M) now stops after the first execution-revert send instead of burning ~6 identical submissions. AA40/validation-OOG retries are unchanged.

## 3. Unsponsored prefund gate (`prefund-gate`)
- Unsponsored fallback is skipped entirely for swap-heavy execution reverts (deterministic — sponsorship isn't the problem).
- When the fallback does apply, `assessUnsponsoredPrefundFeasibility` requires `walletBalance >= callValue + maxGasCost` and surfaces "reduce the amount or keep gas headroom" copy instead of submitting a UserOp guaranteed to revert on the wrap leg.

## 4. Multi-call masking fix (`fix-masking`)
- `simulateSmartWalletCalls` no longer returns `success: true` from the first-leg-only probe when `executeBatch` simulation hits `Unauthorized` on a multi-call batch — it now fails explicitly, so `zoraRouterValidatedBeforeSend` can't be set from a batch whose swap leg was never validated.

## 5. Tests + verification (`tests-verify`)
- 23/23 Vitest tests pass, including new coverage for: failing-leg identification (Permit2 `InvalidNonce` → mapped error), all-passed and indeterminate (infra-failure) replay outcomes, ladder-abort classification, prefund feasibility math, the Unauthorized multi-call masking fix, and address normalization.
- **Live verification against the canonical CSW on Base mainnet** confirmed the replay shape works end-to-end: a 1-wei wrap and a 0.049 ETH wrap pass as `executeBatch` self-calls, while a wrap exceeding balance correctly reverts and is identified as the failing leg.
- `pnpm typecheck` and ESLint (`--max-warnings=0`) are clean.
